{D147}]rgya gar skad du/_Ar+ya ta thA ga ta ma hA ka ru NA ni ra de sha nA ma ma hA yA na sU tra/_bod skad du/_'phags pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po nges par bstan pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo/_bam po dang po/_sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems 
[\[142a.2\]]dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo/_/'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/_bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i khab na bya rgod kyi phung po'i ri/_sngon rgyal ba bzhugs bzhugs pa'i mchod rten chen po de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyis brlabs pa sa'i snying po/_
[\[142a.3\]]byang chub sems dpas mngon par bstod pa/_chos kyi gdan lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_dri za dang /_lha ma yin gyis phyag byas pa'i sa phyogs/_dge ba'i rtsa ba yang dag par skul ba/_de bzhin gshegs pa 'byung ba chos kyi sgo mngon par brjod pa/_byang chub sems 
[\[142a.4\]]dpa' 'byung ba/_yon tan tshad med pa las yang dag par byung ba/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul na/_bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid ni mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas/_chos kyi 'khor lo ni shin tu bskor/_slob ma shin tu dul ba mtha' yas pa'i 
[\[142a.5\]]tshogs ni mnga'/_chos thams cad la mnga' dbang ni brnyes/_sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa sgrub pa ni legs par mkhyen/_dbang po dam pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa ni brnyes/_sems can thams cad kyi bag chags kyi mtshams sbyor ba legs par 'joms pa la ni mkhas/_
[\[142a.6\]]sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa lhun gyis grub pa ni rgyun mi 'chad cing dge slong stong phrag drug cu'i dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang thabs cig tu bzhugs te/_thams cad kyang cang shes pa/_glang po chen po/_bya ba byas pa/_byed pa byas pa/_khur bor ba/_bdag gi don rjes su 
[\[142a.7\]]thob pa/_srid par kun tu sbyor ba yongs su zad pa/_yang dag pa'i shes pas sems shin tu rnam par grol ba/_shes rab shin tu rnam par grol ba/_nyon mongs pa'i bag chags kyi mtshams sbyor ba thams cad legs par bcom pa/_de bzhin gshegs pa chos kyi rgyal 
[\[142b\]]
[\[142b.1\]]po'i sras/_chos zab mo la gnas par mkhas pa/_dmigs pa med pa'i chos kyis nges par byung ba/_spyod lam mdzes pa phun sum tshogs pa/_sbyin pa'i gnas chen por gyur pa/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i bka' la shin tu zhugs pa sha stag go/_/byang chub sems 
[\[142b.2\]]dpa'i dge 'dun dpag tu med pa dang yang thabs cig ste/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po thams cad mkhyen pa chags pa med pa'i spyod yul gyis nges par byung ba/_byams pa chen po dang /_snying rje chen po'i chos kyi sprin las lhag mthong dang /_rig pa dang /_rnam par 
[\[142b.3\]]grol ba'i glog phyung zhing bdud rtsi'i chu'i rgyun gyi char 'bebs pas 'gro ba thams cad tshim par byed pa/_sems can thams cad la sems sa dang mnyam pa rjes su chags pa dang /_khong khro ba dang bral ba/_byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos sna tshogs kyi lo thog yang dag par 'phel bar byed 
[\[142b.4\]]pa/_nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor bas lhag pa'i shes rab kyi 'od zer rab tu gtong bas gti mug gi mun pa sel ba'i lam gyi snang ba thob pa kun tu ston pa dang /_sems can gyi pad ma kha 'byed pa dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su smin par byed pa dang /_sred pa'i chu skems pa dang /_shes 
[\[142b.5\]]pa'i las kyi mtha' la rab tu 'jog pa la mngon par shes pa chen po dang /_zhi gnas kyis rgyu ba/_zla ba lta bur 'gro ba thams cad la sems mnyam pas mngon par zhi bar bya ba'i bsam pa tshim par bya ba'i thabs la mkhas pas 'grib pa dang 'phel ba kun tu ston pa/_ri rab lta 
[\[142b.6\]]bu'i lhag pa'i bsam pas tshangs pa'i gnas pa'i rin po che shin tu brtan te/_ngos bzhi nas yul gyi rlung thams cad kyis mi sgul cing 'jig rten gyi chos las mngon par 'phags pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kun tu bstan pa snang bar gyur pa/_rgya mtsho chen po ltar gzungs dang dran pa'i snod 
[\[142b.7\]]rgya chen po chos kyi chus yongs su gang ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa rin po ches 'gro ba thams cad nye bar 'tsho ba/[ {]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]kyi rgyal po ltar bden pa'i rkang pa dang /_byams pa rab tu 'phel ba'i mjug ma dang /_zhi gnas dang lhag mthong gi mig dang ldan zhing chos zab 
[\[143a\]]
[\[143a.1\]]mo la skrag pa med pas pha rol rgol ba thams cad 'joms pa/_bskal pa brgya stong phrag dpag tu med cing mtha' yas thug pa med par byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa rgyun ma bcad pas yon tan thams cad dang ldan pa sha stag dang thabs cig pa la 'di lta ste/_byang chub sems 
[\[143a.2\]]dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun nas snang zhing sgrib pa med par lta ba zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po zhing thams cad kyi rgyun bkod pa kun tu ston pa zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs las byung 
[\[143a.3\]]ba'i spyod pa'i blo gros zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyod lam thams cad kyis 'dul ba mi 'khrugs pa kun tu ston pa zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spobs pa mtha' yas pa'i tog gi rgyal mtshan rnam par sprul pa'i dbyangs 
[\[143a.4\]]zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gser bzang po rnam par bsags pa'i 'od kyi gzi brjid kyi 'od zer zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyis rnam par nges pa'i tshig la spobs pa zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems 
[\[143a.5\]]dpa' chen po bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs dpag tu med pas brtan pas bsags pa zhes bya ba dang /_de la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po grangs med dpag tu med pa dag dang thabs cig go/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la 'dug 
[\[143a.6\]]pas nges par 'byung ba sgrib pa med pa'i sgo zhes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs yang dag par rab tu ston to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas nas lo bcu drug bzhes te/_tshangs par spyod pa rgyas par gyur par mkhyen/_byang chub sems dpa' 'dus pa chen po 
[\[143a.7\]]de yang gzigs shing mkhyen te/_kye ma byang chub sems dpa' 'dus pa 'di dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos kyi mdzod 'dzin par mkhyen nas 'di snyam du gang gi phyir ngas byang chub sems dpa' rgya cher rjes su yongs su gzung ba dang /_'di lta ste/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
[\[143b\]]
[\[143b.1\]]chen po rnams de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la gzud pa'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa chen po dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i cho 'phrul chen pos byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la 'jug pas nges par 'byung ba sgrib pa med pa'i sgo zhes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs yang dag 
[\[143b.2\]]par bstan par bya'o snyam du dgongs so/_/de nas de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa'i ting nge 'dzin sangs rgyas kyi yul rnam par sprul pa ji lta ba bzhin du yang dag par ston pa zhes bya ba la snyoms par zhugs so/_/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa'i ting nge 'dzin 
[\[143b.3\]]sangs rgyas kyi yul rnam par sprul pa ji lta ba bzhin du yang dag par ston pa zhes bya ba de la snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu de nas de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi mthus steng gi bar snang la 'dod pa'i khams dang /_gzugs kyi khams kyi bar du 'khor gyi khyams/_sangs rgyas kyi gnas de bzhin 
[\[143b.4\]]gshegs pa'i dge ba'i rtsa bas mngon par grub pa/_byang chub sems dpa'i bsam pa yongs su sbyong ba/_phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams snang bar byed pa/_sems can tshad med pa'i bsam pa tshim par byed pa/_lha'i gzhal med khang thams cad zil gyis gnon pa/_phyogs 
[\[143b.5\]]bcu'i byang chub sems dpa' yang dag par skul ba/_gzhi bai dUr+ya dkar po'i dbyibs dang /_'dzam bu'i gling gi gser gyi khang pa dag dang /_dpal gyi snying po'i rta babs dag dang /_rin po che rdo'i snying po'i sgo khang dag dang /_nor bu rin po che snying po'i kha khyer dag dang /_rin 
[\[143b.6\]]po che dri ma med pa'i 'od kyi stegs bu dag dang /_rin po che kun nas 'od kyi khang pa brtsegs pa dag dang /_rin po che thams cad kyis dgab pa dang /_mu tig gi rgyan phreng rab tu spyangs pa dang /_gdugs dang /_rgyal mtshan dang /_ba dan sgreng ba dang /_lha'i dar gyi lda ldi mngon par 
[\[143b.7\]]spyangs pas bsngags pa bstan pa dpag tu med cing thug pa med pa stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi tshad tsam zhig byung ste/_'khor gyi khyams de yang tsan dan sbrul gyi snying pos bskus pa/_tsan dan dus kyi rjes su 'brang ba'i dri yid du 'ong ba dang ldan pa/_dri'i 
[\[144a\]]
[\[144a.1\]]mchog a ga ru dam pa'i snying pos bdugs pa/_klu'i mu tig dang rin po che'i me tog gis rnam par spras pa/_me tog sil ma bkram pa/_rin po che'i shing dang /_rin po che'i mkha' rten gyis mdzes par byas pa ste/_phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad 
[\[144a.2\]]na rgyan mdzes pa bkod pa ji snyed pa de dag thams cad 'khor gyi khyams de nyid na kun tu snang ngo /_/der gdan seng ge'i khri bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag mang po dpag tu med pa/_grangs med pa/_sna tshogs pa/_blta na sdug pa/_mtho ba/_rab tu bzang ba/_yangs pa/_rgya che ba/_
[\[144a.3\]]yid du 'ong ba/_yid dga' bar byed pa dag byung bar gyur to/_/gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'dir them skas kha khyer mi bdun gyi 'phang tsam gyis bskor ba/_rgyar dpag tshad phyed kyis yongs su bzung ba/_rin po che an da rnyil dang /_mthon kha chen pos snying por gyur pa/_brgya 
[\[144a.4\]]byin thogs pa'i nor bu rin po che chen po'i gdugs can/_tshangs pa'i ne tso 'am ci dang /_rin po che ser pos spras pa/_rin po che'i dril bu g.yer kha'i dra ba kun tu bskyod pas sgra 'byung ba/_sa'i steng nas 'khor gyi khyams la thug gi bar du gyen du mngon par 'phags pa/_drang ba ma yo ba 
[\[144a.5\]]bzhi stong byung bar gyur to/_/gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'dir ji lta ba bzhin du stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du yang de lta bu'i them skas chen po dag byung bar gyur to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das dgongs 
[\[144a.6\]]shing mkhyen bzhin du ting nge 'dzin de las bzhengs par gyur to/_/bcom ldan 'das ting nge 'dzin de las bzhengs ma thag tu stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di rnam pa drug tu g.yos te/_snang ba chen po yang byung bar gyur to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems 
[\[144a.7\]]dpa'i tshogs dang thabs cig_/nyan thos kyi tshogs dang thabs cig tu byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs kyis yongs su bskor cing nyan thos kyi tshogs kyis mdun du bdar te/_lha dang klus mngon par bstod cing me tog gi char chen po ni 'bebs/_na bza'i char chen pos ni mngon par 'gebs/_
[\[144b\]]
[\[144b.1\]]phye ma dang /_/spos dang /_byug pa'i char chen po ni 'thor/_sil snyan dang /_sgra dbyangs chen po ni rab tu sgrogs/_'jig rten gyi khams dpag tu med pa ni rab tu g.yo bar mdzad/_'jig rten gyi khams grangs med pa ni snang bar mdzad cing sangs rgyas kyi mthu chen po dang /_sangs 
[\[144b.2\]]rgyas kyi rdzu 'phrul chen po dang /_sangs rgyas kyi cho 'phrul chen po dang /_sangs rgyas kyi khyu mchog tu gyur pa chen po dang /_sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs chen po dang /_sangs rgyas kyi gzi brjid chen po dang /_sangs rgyas kyi byin chen po dang /_sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa chen 
[\[144b.3\]]pos bya rgod kyi phung po'i ri las mi snang bar gyur te/_lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_dri za dang /_lha ma yin dang /_nam mkha' lding dang /_mi 'am ci dang /_lto 'phye chen po bye ba phrag du ma zhabs 'bring du 'brang zhing nor bu rin po che sna tshogs kyi them skas kyi dbus nas 
[\[144b.4\]]'khor gyi khyams bkod pa ga la ba der gshegs so/_/de nas rgyal po chen po bzhi la sogs pa rgyal chen bzhi'i ris kyi lha rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das them skas su zhugs par rig nas bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa'i las kyi phyir lha'i me tog gi sprin gyi char rab tu phab ste 
[\[144b.5\]]thal mo sbyar nas bcom ldan 'das la mngon par bstod pa/_nyi zla me dang nor bu'i 'od dang lha yul pa yi lha 'od shin tu dri med pa/_/thub pas 'od kyis thams cad zil gyis mnan nas ngan song gsum po dag kyang zhi bar mdzad/_/ri bo lhun po dang ni kho ra khor yug rtse mo 
[\[144b.6\]]bsnyil te zhing rnams gsal bar gyur pas na/_/dga' zhing yid rnams mgu nas don rnams thams cad grub pa'i rgyal ba la ni skyabs su mchi/_/de nas rgyal chen bzhi'i ris kyi lha rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'dis mngon par bstod nas bcom ldan 'das 
[\[144b.7\]]gshegs pa'i zhabs 'bring du 'brang ngo /_/de nas lha'i dbang po brgya byin la sogs pa sum cu rtsa gsum gyi lha rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das them skas su zhugs par rig nas bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa'i las kyi phyir lha'i spos kyi sprin gyi char rab tu phab ste/_thal mo sbyar nas bcom 
[\[145a\]]
[\[145a.1\]]ldan 'das la mngon par bstod pa/_rdzu 'phrul dam pa brnyes shing rab tu mtshungs pa med pa rnam par 'phrul pa shin tu ston/_/rgyal ba nyi ma ji bzhin 'phags te zhing rnams bye ba brgya dag tu yang rab tu gda'/_/rgyal bas rang gi yon tan kun gyis zhing rnams 
[\[145a.2\]]thams cad brgyan te shin tu mdzes mdzad pa/_/gang zhig shin tu mnyam med mtshungs pa med cing rdzu 'phrul dbang du gyur la phyag 'tshal lo/_/de nas sum cu rtsa gsum gyi lha rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'dis mngon par bstod nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs 'bring 
[\[145a.3\]]du 'brang ngo /_/de nas lha'i rgyal po rab 'thab bral la sogs pa 'thab bral gyi lha rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das them skas su zhugs par rig nas bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa'i las kyi phyir lha'i na bza'i sprin gyi char rab tu phab ste thal mo sbyar nas bcom ldan 'das la mngon par 
[\[145a.4\]]bstod pa/_bde gshegs khyod kyi mkhyen pa mtha' yas yangs pas sems can bsam pa mkhyen/_/mkha' 'dra'i lam gyi chos gsum dag la chags med thams cad mkhyen pa nyid/_/sems kyi 'jug pa gcig gis 'gro ba'i sems kyi spyod pa thams cad mkhyen/_/des 
[\[145a.5\]]na srid pa gsum po dag na lus can rnams kyis mchod par 'os/_/de nas 'thab bral gyi lha rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'dis mngon par bstod nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs 'bring du 'brang bar gyur to/_/de nas lha'i rgyal po rab dga' ldan la sogs 
[\[145a.6\]]pa dga' ldan gyi lha rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das them skas su zhugs par rig nas bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa'i las kyi phyir lha'i rin po che'i sprin gyi char rab tu phab ste thal mo sbyar nas bcom ldan 'das la mngon par bstod pa/_gang gi slad du sgra dang yi ge ma mchis gang du'ang rgyu ba 
[\[145a.7\]]ma mchis pas/_/khyod kyis chos rnams mi g.yo ya ma brla dang sgyu ma smig rgyu 'drar mkhyen nas/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis thugs rjes sems can rnams la ched du chos mchog gsungs pas na/_/chos kyi dngos po'i rang bzhin mkhas pa bdag med gsung ba'i rgyal la phyag 'tshal lo/_/
[\[145b\]]
[\[145b.1\]]de nas dga' ldan gyi ris kyi lha rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'dis mngon par bstod nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs 'bring du 'brang ngo /_/de nas lha'i rgyal po rab 'phrul dga' la sogs pa 'phrul dga'i lha rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das them skas su zhugs par 
[\[145b.2\]]rig nas/_bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa'i las kyi phyir lha'i rgyan gyi sprin gyi char rab tu phab ste/_thal mo sbyar nas bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'dis mngon par bstod do/_/chos kyi zong gis rang gi stobs bcu mkha' 'dra 'od zer ldan pa dang /_/thugs rje brnyes 
[\[145b.3\]]nas byams pa'i dgongs pas sems can rnams la gzugs ston pa/_/gru tshul chos la gnyis su med par zhugs kyang rgyal ba mtshungs par mkhyen/_/chos kyi khyad par sems lhag don kun grub pa'i rgyal la phyag 'tshal lo/_/de nas 'phrul dga'i lha rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das 
[\[145b.4\]]la tshigs su bcad pa 'dis mngon par bstod nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs 'bring du 'brang bar gyur to/_/de nas lha'i rgyal po dbang sgyur la sogs pa gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi lha rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das them skas su zhugs pa dang /_'khor gyi khyams su mngon par phyogs par 
[\[145b.5\]]rig nas bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa'i las kyi phyir mu tig gi do shal gyi sprin gyi char rab tu phab ste/_thal mo sbyar nas bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis mngon par bstod do/_/tshul khrims dri ma mi mnga' rnam dag rnam par nges pa dam pa ting nge 'dzin mchog mi 
[\[145b.6\]]g.yos pa/_/mnyam med blo ldan rgya mtsho 'dra bar gnas shing srid las rnam grol dbang sgyur zhabs la phyag 'tshal lo/_/rim gyis byams dang ldan pa'i thugs rje yis ni bden pa'i lam gyi rnam pa rab tu gnas mdzad pa/_/rnam thar gsum gyi lam gyi khyams na gnas pa'i bde 
[\[145b.7\]]gshegs seng ge khyod la bdag cag phyag 'tshal lo/_/snying rje'i chu bo ting nge 'dzin gyis rnam 'phel ba/_/zhi gnas 'dab ma rnam par grol ba'i me tog rgyas/_/slob ma'i tshogs kyi bung bas nges par bsnyen gyur pa/_/bde gshegs pad ma la ni spyi bos phyag 'tshal lo/_/mnga' 
[\[146a\]]
[\[146a.1\]]bdag g.yo ba ma mchis tshul khrims sar gnas pas/_/brtson 'grus mtshungs pa ma mchis stobs kyis yang dag 'phags/_/yul gyi rlung rnams langs pa chen pos mi g.yos pa/_/sangs rgyas ri bo dam pa khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/mnga' bdag thugs rjes 
[\[146a.2\]]dbyangs kyi dkyil 'khor rnam dag pa/_/blo gter blo mchog gis ni rab tu 'joms mdzad pa/_/sems can brgya dag gis ni mang pos mchod gyur pa/_/bde gshegs zla ba khyod la bdag cag phyag 'tshal lo/_/mkha' ltar zhi ba'i rnam par thar pa'i lam gshegs pa/_/shes 
[\[146a.3\]]rab 'od kyis phyogs bcu dag ni rgyas 'gengs shing /_/skye bo mang po mtshungs med pad ma kha 'byed pa/_/sangs rgyas nyi ma dam pa khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/mnga' bdag phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gsum po dag na yang /_/'dren pa khyod dang mnyam pa dmigs par ma gyur te/_/khyod 
[\[146a.4\]]ni yon tan kun gyis yang dag grub pa lags/_/bdag cag khyod la bstod pas ngoms par 'gyur ma lags/_/lha dbang klu dang lha ma yin gyis phyag bgyis pa/_/lha mchog brgya dag gis kyang yang dag mngon bstod pa/_/bsngags pa brgya phrag mang pos yang dag mngon bstod pa/_/
[\[146a.5\]]bde bar gshegs pa bstod 'os khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/de nas gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi lha rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis mngon par bstod nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs 'bring du 'brang ngo /_/de nas bcom ldan 'das de ltar sangs rgyas kyi 
[\[146a.6\]]yul rnam par sprul pa tshad med pa lha 'bum gyis mngon par bstod nas 'khor gyi khyams rin po che bkod par gshegs te/_ji ltar bcom ldan 'das gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams mi mjed 'dir them skas nas 'khor gyi khyams rin po che bkod par gshegs pa shes pa de 
[\[146a.7\]]bzhin du/_stong gsum gyi 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad na yang gshegs par shes so/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das 'khor gyi khyams stong gsum gyis yongs su gzung ba rin po che bkod par gdan seng ge'i khri ji ltar bshams pa 
[\[146b\]]
[\[146b.1\]]la bzhugs so/_/byang chub sems dpa' dang nyan thos chen po dag kyang stan ci rigs pa la 'khod par gyur to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi ting nge 'dzin rnam par thar pa sgrib pa med pa la sangs rgyas rnam par rol pa zhes bya ba la snyoms par zhugs so/_/bcom

[\[232b.2\]]khrag khrig dag kyang mthong 'gyur zhing /_/bla na med pa'i chos kyang nyan par byed/_/rgya chen chos ni thos par gyur nas kyang /_/gzungs kyi dran pa'i stobs kyis 'dzin par byed/_/don dang tshig 'bru dag kyang gzungs ldan par/_/sems can thams cad la ni ston par byed/_/
[\[232b.3\]]rgyu dang rkyen gyis rnam par phye bas na/_/sems ni skad cig 'jig par shes gyur te/_/chos rnams rab tu dbye ba shes thob pa/_/gzungs 'di la ni gnas par gyur pa yin/_/spobs pa thob cing gsum rig chags pa med/_/mig gsum rnam par dag pa'ang rab tu 
[\[232b.4\]]thob/_/rnam par thar pa gsum gyi don kyang thob/_/'dir ni gzungs rnams thob par gyur dang ldan/_/gang dag gzungs rnams mang po gzhal yas shing /_/tshad med pa dag nges par bstan pa yang /_/gzungs kyi dam pa 'di ni thob gyur na/_/de dag thams 
[\[232b.5\]]cad rab tu thob par 'gyur/_/bsam gtan gang dang rnam thar gang dag dang /_/snyoms 'jug ting 'dzin gang dag mtha' yas dang /_/mngon par shes pas rnam par rol dang gzungs/_/mtha' yas de dag la ni rab tu 'jug_/ji ltar rgya mtsho dpag tu med pa la/_/
[\[232b.6\]]mtha' yas sgo nas klung mtsho mtshe'u 'jug pa/_/de ltar gzungs 'di 'dzin par byed pa yang /_/mtha' yas chos rnams kyi ni sgo mor 'jug_/zad pa med pa'i sgra la 'jug pa yang /_/zad pa med pa'i shes pas khong du chud/_/gzungs 'di smra bar byed pa de 
[\[232b.7\]]yi ni/_/bsod nams thams cad zad par mi 'gyur ro/_/gzungs 'di rab tu thob par gyur pa ni/_/rin chen dam pa dri med lag tu 'gyur/_/gzugs dang mtshan rnams dang ni rigs dang yang /_/rus dang skye ba dag ni rnam par dag_/zab mo'i tshul 
[\[233a\]]
[\[233a.1\]]dag la ni 'jug pa dang /_/mi skye ba la bzod dang ma skyes dang /_/phyir mi ldog pa'i shes pa'i sa dag kyang /_/gzungs 'di yin par nges par bstan pa yin/_/byang chub bla med mchog 'di tshol byed pa'i/_/byang chub sems dpa' mang po gang 
[\[233a.2\]]dag gis/_/gzungs 'di rab tu thob par gyur na ni/_/byang chub bla na med pa rnyed mi dka'/_/gang dag phyogs bcu rnams na bde bar gshegs/_/'gro la phan dang bde phyir chos ston pa/_/gzungs kyi dam pa 'di ni thob gyur na/_/de dag spobs pa rgyun chad mi 'gyur 
[\[233a.3\]]ro/_/gzungs 'di rab tu thob par gyur pa dag_/dga' dang mos dang bsam pa shes nas ni/_/de dag 'bras bu yod pa ston pas na/_/sems can khrag khrig dag ni tshim par byed/_/gzungs 'di la ni gnas pa'i dpa' bo dag_/'khor lo dam pa rab tu skor byed 
[\[233a.4\]]cing /_/sems can sdug bsngal sna tshogs rnams las 'grol/_/theg pa dam pa la yang 'jog par byed/_/bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig mang por ni/_/bsngags dang yon tan legs pa brjod byas kyang /_/drang srong chen pos de yi yon tan gyi/_/mtha' ma brjod du 
[\[233a.5\]]nus par mi 'gyur ro/_/rigs kyi bu de ltar bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dri ma med par snang ba des gzungs bstan pa 'di bshad pa na/_byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor chen po de las byang chub sems dpa' stong phrag sum cu rtsa gnyis kyis gzungs thob par gyur to/_/
[\[233a.6\]]byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'od dpal gyis ni rin chen sgron ma'i gzungs 'di thob par gyur to/_/gzungs kyi dbang phyug gi rgyal po khyod de'i tshe de'i dus na/_byang chub sems dpa' 'od dpal zhes bya bar gyur pa de gzhan zhig yin pa snyam du dogs shing yid 
[\[233a.7\]]gnyis su gyur tam/_the tshom za ba lta na/_khyod kyis de ltar mi blta'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_khyod nyid de'i tshe de'i dus na byang chub sems dpa' 'od dpal zhes bya bar gyur te/_gzungs kyi dbang phyug gi rgyal po khyod kyis de tshun chad bgrang ba las 'das pa'i 
[\[233b\]]
[\[233b.1\]]de bzhin gshegs pa dag la gzungs rnam par nges pa 'di yongs su dris so/_/yongs su dri ba byas so/_/des na khyod da ltar yang de bzhin gshegs pa la yongs su 'dri ba de ltar 'jigs pa med cing gzungs kyi rnam par nges pa 'di'ang ston to/_/khyod kun du lta ba'i 
[\[233b.2\]]blos chos 'di mngon sum du gyur pas rnam grangs 'dis na ngas khyod byang chub sems dpa' gzungs thob pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' dran pa dang ldan pa rnams kyi mchog tu bstan to/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' shes rab brtsegs zhes bya ba 'khor de nyid du 'dug par 
[\[233b.3\]]gyur pa des/_bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rin chen sgron ma'i gzungs 'di rab tu thob cing /_thob nas kyang chab 'tshal bar mi bgyid pa dang /_'di lta ste/_gzungs kyi stobs bskyed pas sems 
[\[233b.4\]]can thams cad nye bar 'tsho ba lags/_de skad ces gsol pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' shes rab brtsegs la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/rigs kyi bu gang gi tshe byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi rtsa ba la gnas par gyur pa dang /_shes 
[\[233b.5\]]rab kyi las byed par gyur pa de'i tshe gzungs 'di rab tu 'thob bo/_/chud za bar mi byed do/_/sems can thams cad kyi nye bar 'tsho bar yang 'gyur ro/_/de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' shes rab brtsegs kyis 'di skad ces 
[\[233b.6\]]gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das shes rab kyi rtsa ba ni gang lags/_shes rab kyi las ni gang lags/_de bzhin gshegs pas legs par bshad du gsol/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_de'i phyir rigs kyi bu khyod shin tu legs par nyon 
[\[233b.7\]]la yid la zung shig dang /_ngas khyod la bshad par bya'o/_/rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' ma thos pa'i chos nyan pa gang yin pa 'di ni shes rab kyi rtsa ba'o/_/ji ltar thos pa'i chos gzhan dag la rgya cher yang dag par ston pa gang yin pa 'di ni shes rab kyi las 
[\[234a\]]
[\[234a.1\]]so/_/dpyod pa ni shes rab kyi rtsa ba'o/_/gang dpyad pa gzhan dag la nye bar ston pa 'di ni shes rab kyi las so/_/tshul bzhin so sor rtog pa ni shes rab kyi rtsa ba'o/_/sems can rnams tshul bzhin la rab tu dgod pa 'di ni shes rab kyi las so/_/

[\[234a.6\]]rdzu 'phrul thob pa ni shes rab kyi las so/_/dad pas mos te yid ches pa ni shes rab kyi rtsa ba'o/_/chags pa thams cad las yang dag par 'da' ba ni shes rab kyi las so/_/sems mi 'jum pa ni shes rab kyi rtsa ba'o/_/lus dang sems shin tu sbyangs pa ni shes rab

[\[237b\]]
[\[237b.1\]]pa 'di dag gsungs so/_/ma mos gyur nas ma thos chos rnams rtag nyan pa/_/shes rab rtsa ba sems can dag pas thob nas ni/_/'dzin cing byams pas 'gro ba dag la ston byed pa/_/shes rab las te byang chub sems dpa' rtag tu byed/_/dpyod pa'i dran 
[\[237b.2\]]pa chen po 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/dpyad nas 'gro la rtag tu ston pa shes rab las/_/gal te tshul bzhin rab sreg 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/de la sbyar nas ston pa shes rab las zhes bya/_/yang dag sbyor la gal te sbyor ba shes rab rtsa/_/sgrub cing ston par byed pa de 
[\[237b.3\]]ni shes rab las/_/sems la nges par sems rnyed 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/nges par sems pa spyod pa 'di ni shes rab las/_/gcig pu dben par spyod pa 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/lus dang sems la nga'i med 'di ni shes rab las/_/gang tshe 'du 'dzi'i 'jigs 
[\[237b.4\]]gyur de ni shes rab rtsa/_/gcig pu bgrod pa'i lam la rig byed shes rab las/_/zhi gnas lhag mthong dga' ba 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/rig dang rnam par grol la reg 'di shes rab las/_/rnam thar gsum la mngon par brtson pa shes rab rtsa/_/rig gsum mngon 
[\[237b.5\]]du byed pa shes rab las zhes bya/_/gal te dran pa nyer gzhag sgom pa shes rab rtsa/_/dran med yid la byed med de ni shes rab las/_/sdig pa spangs te dge ba spyod pa shes rab rtsa/_/chos 'di rang bzhin spangs pa de ni shes rab las/_/gal te rdzu 'phrul rkang pa bzhi 
[\[237b.6\]]sgom shes rab rtsa/_/lhun grub rdzu 'phrul thob pa 'di ni shes rab las/_/dad pas mos nas yid ches pa ni shes rab rtsa/_/chags pa thams cad gang bral de ni shes rab las/_/zhum med rtag tu brtson pa de ni shes rab rtsa/_/lus dang yid sems rab sbyangs de ni shes rab las/_/
[\[237b.7\]]dran dang shes bzhin mkhas pa 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/gnas rnams kun la mi gnas 'di ni shes rab las/_/rang gis ting 'dzin rtogs pa de ni shes rab rtsa/_/rang bzhin mnyam par bzhag la spyod 'di shes rab las/_/gal te shes rab dbang por rab gnas shes rab rtsa/_/
[\[238a\]]
[\[238a.1\]]'gro ba'i dbang po thams cad shes pa shes rab las/_/stobs lnga po dag rjes su sgom pa shes rab rtsa/_/bcom nas ye shes thob pa de ni shes rab las/_/byang chub yan lag rjes 'thun bzod pa shes rab rtsa/_/chos kun rjes su gang rtogs de ni shes rab las/_/
[\[238a.2\]]lam gyi yan lag skye gnas sgrub pa shes rab rtsa/_/chos dang chos min spong ba 'di ni shes rab las/_/sdug bsngal rang bzhin mkhas pa 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/gal te 'gog pa mngon sum byed 'di shes rab las/_/drang ba'i don gyi mdo sde tshig 'dzin shes rab rtsa/_/nges pa'i 
[\[238a.3\]]don la rton pa 'di ni shes rab las/_/thams cad thos pas mi ngoms 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/don la rab tu rton pa 'di ni shes rab las/_/rnam par shes la tshul bzhin tshol ba shes rab rtsa/_/ye shes la ni rab tu rton 'di shes rab las/_/gang zag gang la'ang ma 
[\[238a.4\]]zhen 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/gal te chos nyid rton pa 'di ni shes rab las/_/'du byed mi rtag yongs su tshol ba shes rab rtsa/_/gal te gzod nas ma skyes shes pa shes rab las/_/'du byed sdug bsngal mos par byed pa shes rab rtsa/_/chos rnams 'dus ma byas su mos pa shes 
[\[238a.5\]]rab las/_/chos rnams bdag med the tshom med pa shes rab rtsa/_/sems can 'di dag gdod nas dag de shes rab las/_/mya ngan 'das la zhi bar mos pa shes rab rtsa/_/'gro ba shin tu mya ngan 'das 'di shes rab las/_/don gyi rjes su rtogs pa 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/don rtogs so so 
[\[238a.6\]]yang dag rig pa shes rab las/_/gal te chos nyid mos par byed pa shes rab rtsa/_/chos la so so yang dag rig pa shes rab las/_/sgra rnams kun la skrag pa med 'di shes rab rtsa/_/nges tshig so so yang dag rig 'di shes rab las/_/skrag med blo yis spobs pa de ni shes rab rtsa/_/
[\[238a.7\]]spobs pa so so yang dag rig pa shes rab las/_/gal te sems can gnas pa'i byams de shes rab rtsa/_/dmigs pa med pa'i byams thob de ni shes rab las/_/bdag dang gzhan la snying rje 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/gang gis gnyi gar mi rtog de ni shes rab las/_/mchog tu 
[\[238b\]]
[\[238b.1\]]dga' la dga' ba thob 'di shes rab rtsa/_/dga' med gdung ba med pa 'di ni shes rab las/_/byams dang gnod par mi byed de ni shes rab rtsa/_/gnyis las sems grol thob pa de ni shes rab las/_/blo gros ldan pa sangs rgyas dran 'di shes rab rtsa/_/gang tshe chos sku rjes su 
[\[238b.2\]]rtogs pa shes rab las/_/chos kyang rtag tu dran pa 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/chos rnams 'dod chags bral bar rtogs pa shes rab las/_/yon tan las ni dge 'dun dran de shes rab rtsa/_/de yang 'dus ma byas su rtogs pa shes rab las/_/blo ldan gtong ba dran pa de ni shes rab rtsa/_/
[\[238b.3\]]gang phyir nyon mongs kun 'dor 'di ni shes rab las/_/tshul khrims shin tu dag pa dran 'di shes rab rtsa/_/tshul khrims zag pa med la gnas ni shes rab las/_/che ba'i bdag nyid lha dran 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/dag nas dag pa dran pa 'di ni shes rab las/_/gang phyir bsgrims 
[\[238b.4\]]te thos pa tshol ba shes rab rtsa/_/gzhan dag las ni phyis mi tshol ba shes rab las/_/legs par byed pa'i las byed 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/las phyis mngon par 'du mi byed pa shes rab las/_/nga rgyal med cing 'dud par byed pa shes rab rtsa/_/ye shes che ba'i bdag nyid thob 
[\[238b.5\]]'di shes rab las/_/bdag gi don la brtson 'gyur 'di ni shes rab rtsa/_/sems can rnams kyi don byed 'di ni shes rab las/_/gal te chos kyi phung po 'dzin byed shes rab rtsa/_/sems can spyod pa rjes su rtogs byed shes rab las/_/gzhan gyi blo la yang dag thob byed shes 
[\[238b.6\]]rab rtsa/_/sems can theg gsum nges 'byung byed pa shes rab las/_/blo gros ldan pa sbyin dang snyan par smra ba dang /_/don byed sems can yongs su smin byed shes rab rtsa/_/gal te don 'thun pa yis sangs rgyas yon tan 'god/_/'di ni skyes bu dam pa rnams kyi shes 
[\[238b.7\]]rab las/_/srid pa kun du 'bar shes skrag pa shes rab rtsa/_/mi skye bzod pa thob pa de ni shes rab las/_/phyir mi ldog pa'i sa 'di shes rab rtsa ba ste/_/dbang bskur de yi sa thob de ni shes rab las/_/gal te byang chub shing drung 'dug pa shes rab rtsa/_/kun 
[\[239a\]]
[\[239a.1\]]mkhyen ye shes reg pa 'di ni shes rab las/_/byang chub sems 'di shes rab rtsa ba zhes kyang bya/_/sems ni gnas su gang byed de ni shes rab las/_/gang dag byang chub sems la rtag gnas mi g.yo ba/_/de dag shes rab kyis ni rtag tu las bya byed/_/rtsa

[\[239a.3\]]byung /_/gang dag ma 'ongs pa dang phyogs bcu'i thub dbang po/_/tshad med pa la bsnyen bkur tshad med byed 'dod gang /_/de ni ma myos byang chub sems la dga' ba yin/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' gzungs kyi dbang phyug gi rgyal pos 'di skad ces gsol 
[\[239a.4\]]to/_/bcom ldan 'das/_kye ma ji tsam du sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub 'di shin tu rnam par nges shing /_yi ge rnams kyis bstan pa mtha' yas pa/_don gyis 'jug pa mtha' yas pa/_mi brtson pa rnams kyis 
[\[239a.5\]]'jug par dka' ba/_rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba rjes su rtogs pas zab pa/_gnyis la spyod pa rnams kyis 'tshal bar dka' ba/_skye mched drug la brten pa rnams kyis mthong bar dka' ba/_theg pa dman pa la mos pa rnams kyis yid ches par dka' ba/_nyan 
[\[239a.6\]]thos dang rang sangs rgyas rnams kyis ma lags pa/_byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi spyod yul/_chos thams cad kyi phyag rgya/_chos kyi dbyings mnyam pa nyid pas phye ba ma mchis pa/_nam mkha'i mtshan nyid kyi slad du 'gyur ba ma mchis pa/_shin tu 'das pas kun

[\[242a.7\]]'dzam bu'i gling gi mi rnams la chos kyi rnam grangs 'di yun ring du gnas par gyur cig_/dge slong dang /_dge slong ma dang /_dge bsnyen dang /_dge bsnyen ma gang dag mdo sde 'di lta bu 'dzin pa de dag kyang dus ring por yun du tshe yongs su skyong bar gyur cig_/sems can 
[\[242b\]]
[\[242b.1\]]thams cad la yang phan pa byed par gyur cig_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' gzungs kyi dbang phyug gi rgyal pos 'di skad ces gsol to/_/kye ma bcom ldan 'das ji tsam du rnam par nges pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs 'di don bzang ba dang /_tshig 'bru bzang ba dang /_
[\[242b.2\]]legs par brgyan pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' thams cad mchog tu dga' ba skyed pa dang /_bdud dang pha rol rgol ba thams cad 'joms pa dang /_chos thams cad la mkhas par 'dzin pa dang /_sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa tshim par bgyid pa dang /_theg pa thams 
[\[242b.3\]]cad kyi nges par 'byung ba'i phyir 'byung ba dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yon tan la 'jug pa'i rjes su song ba dang /_thams cad kyis brtson par bgyi ba las gti mug ma mchis par ston pa ngo mtshar to/_/bcom ldan 'das chos kyi rnam grangs 'di'i ming ci lags/_
[\[242b.4\]]'di ji ltar gzung bar bgyi/_de skad ces gsol pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' gzungs kyi dbang phyug gi rgyal po la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/de'i phyir gzungs kyi dbang phyug gi rgyal po khyod kyis chos kyi rnam grangs 'di de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
[\[242b.5\]]snying rje chen po bstan pa zhes bya bar zung shig_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i las don yod pa nyan pa lung bstan pa zhes bya bar yang zung shig_/bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal nas/_byang chub sems dpa' gzungs kyi dbang phyug gi rgyal po dang /_byang chub 
[\[242b.6\]]sems dpa'i tshogs dang /_nyan thos kyi tshogs de dag thams cad dang /_lha dang /_mi dang /_lha ma yin dang /_dri zar bcas pa'i 'jig rten yi rangs te/_bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa la mngon par bstod do//_//'phags pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying 
[\[242b.7\]]rje chen po bstan pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo rdzogs so//_//rgya gar gyi mkhan po shI len dra bo d+hi dang /_zhu chen gyi lots+tsha ba ban de ye shes sdes bsgyur cing zhus te skad gsar chad kyis kyang bcos nas gtan la phab pa//_//
